#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
benchmark/python_orch.py - Reference Python 3.12 Agent Worker
Evaluates CPython VM dynamic interpretation, memory overhead, JSON serialization,
and tool dispatch under workloads identical to Go and Node.js workers.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import os
import re
import sys
import time
import urllib.request

import numpy as np

REPO_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
API_URL = os.environ.get("LLAMA_API_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:8080/v1/chat/completions")
BASE_DIR = os.environ.get("WORKLOAD_DATA_DIR", os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "data", "workload_data"))


def get_rss_mb() -> float:
    try:
        import resource
        rusage = resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_SELF)
        if sys.platform == "darwin":
            return rusage.ru_maxrss / (1024.0 * 1024.0)
        return rusage.ru_maxrss / 1024.0
    except Exception:
        return 0.0


def build_tool_catalog(count: int = 15) -> list[dict]:
    catalog = []
    for i in range(count):
        catalog.append({
            "type": "function",
            "function": {
                "name": f"system_tool_{i}",
                "description": f"Standard system tool descriptor #{i} for agent runtime evaluation.",
                "parameters": {
                    "type": "object",
                    "properties": {
                        "action": {"type": "string", "description": "Action to execute"},
                        "target": {"type": "string", "description": "Target parameter or file path"},
                    },
                    "required": ["action", "target"],
                },
            },
        })
    # Serialization pass
    return json.loads(json.dumps(catalog))


def build_tool_embeddings(n_tools: int = 54) -> np.ndarray:
    dim = 384
    d_idx = np.arange(dim, dtype=np.float64)[None, :]
    i_idx = np.arange(1, n_tools + 1, dtype=np.float64)[:, None]
    mat = np.sin(i_idx * 0.31 + d_idx * 0.13).astype(np.float32)
    norms = np.linalg.norm(mat, axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-8
    return mat / norms


def prune_tools_vector_rag(catalog: list[dict], tool_emb: np.ndarray, tier: str, top_k: int = 5) -> tuple[list[dict], float]:
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    dim = 384
    n_tools = len(catalog)
    if n_tools <= top_k:
        return catalog, 0.0
    tier_seed = {"W1": 1.0, "W2": 2.0, "W3": 3.0, "W4": 4.0}.get(tier, 1.0)

    d_idx = np.arange(dim, dtype=np.float64)
    q_vec = np.cos(tier_seed * 0.37 + d_idx * 0.19).astype(np.float32)
    q_vec = q_vec / (np.linalg.norm(q_vec) + 1e-8)

    scores = np.dot(tool_emb, q_vec)
    top_indices = sorted(int(idx) for idx in np.argsort(scores)[::-1][:top_k])
    pruned = [catalog[idx] for idx in top_indices]
    elapsed_ms = (time.monotonic() - t0) * 1000.0
    return pruned, elapsed_ms


def execute_tool_list_dir(mock_dir: str) -> str:
    entries = sorted(os.listdir(mock_dir))
    files = []
    for name in entries:
        try:
            sz = os.path.getsize(os.path.join(mock_dir, name))
            files.append(f"{name}:{sz}")
        except Exception:
            files.append(f"{name}:0")
    return json.dumps({"count": len(files), "files": files[:10]}, separators=(",", ":"))


def execute_tool_grep_logs(log_file: str) -> tuple[list[float], str]:
    vals: list[float] = []
    pattern = re.compile(r'"subsystem":\s*"UMA_BUS_CONTROLLER"')
    with open(log_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for line in f:
            if pattern.search(line):
                try:
                    obj = json.loads(line)
                    lat = obj.get("latency_ms")
                    if isinstance(lat, (int, float)):
                        vals.append(float(lat))
                except Exception:
                    pass
    summary = json.dumps({"matched_records": len(vals), "sample": vals[:5]}, separators=(",", ":"))
    return vals, summary


def execute_tool_calc_stats(vals: list[float]) -> str:
    n = len(vals)
    if n == 0:
        return "{}"
    sum_val = 0.0
    for v in vals:
        sum_val += v
    mean = sum_val / float(n)
    var = 0.0
    for v in vals:
        var += (v - mean) ** 2
    variance = var / float(n - 1) if n > 1 else 0.0
    return json.dumps({"n": n, "mean": mean, "variance": variance, "stddev": math.sqrt(variance)}, separators=(",", ":"))


def execute_vector_rag(base_dir: str) -> str:
    in_dim = 256
    out_dim = 384
    n_docs = 10000

    w_file = os.path.join(base_dir, "w_proj.bin")
    b_file = os.path.join(base_dir, "b_proj.bin")
    docs_bin = os.path.join(base_dir, "doc_embeddings.bin")

    if not (os.path.exists(w_file) and os.path.exists(b_file) and os.path.exists(docs_bin)):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"FATAL: Required RAG binary datasets missing in {base_dir}")

    w_proj = np.fromfile(w_file, dtype=np.float32).reshape(in_dim, out_dim)
    b_proj = np.fromfile(b_file, dtype=np.float32)
    doc_matrix = np.fromfile(docs_bin, dtype=np.float32).reshape(n_docs, out_dim)

    # 1. Query vector initialization & linear projection
    d_idx = np.arange(in_dim, dtype=np.float32)
    q_raw = np.sin(d_idx * 0.17) * 0.05
    q_proj = np.dot(q_raw, w_proj) + b_proj

    # 2. L2 Normalization of projected query vector
    q_norm = q_proj / (np.linalg.norm(q_proj) + 1e-8)

    # 3. Cosine similarity against all 10,000 document vectors
    scores = np.dot(doc_matrix, q_norm)

    # 4. Descending sort to select Top-5 chunks
    top5_indices = np.argsort(scores)[::-1][:5]

    chunks_file = os.path.join(base_dir, "rag_chunks_10k.json")
    if not os.path.exists(chunks_file):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"FATAL: rag_chunks_10k.json missing in {base_dir}")

    with open(chunks_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        chunks = json.load(f)

    out_lines = []
    for idx in top5_indices:
        text = chunks.get(str(idx), f"[Chunk #{idx:04d}] Context reference document.")
        out_lines.append(text)
    return "\n".join(out_lines) + "\n"


def call_llama_stream(messages: list[dict], tools: list[dict], max_tokens: int) -> tuple[float, float, float, int, int, str, int, str]:
    payload = json.dumps({
        "model": "Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf",
        "messages": messages,
        "tools": tools,
        "max_tokens": max_tokens,
        "temperature": 0.0,
        "seed": 42,
        "stream": True,
        "cache_prompt": os.environ.get("BENCH_CACHE_PROMPT", "false").lower() == "true",
    }, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    req = urllib.request.Request(API_URL, data=payload, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    prompt_ms = 0.0
    predicted_ms = 0.0
    has_server_timings = False
    ttft_ms = 0.0
    first_chunk = True
    token_count = 0
    cached_tokens = 0
    uncached_prompt_tokens = 0
    usage_prompt_tokens = 0
    completion_tokens = 0
    finish_reason = ""
    out_text = []

    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as resp:
        if resp.status != 200:
            raise RuntimeError(f"FATAL: llama-server returned HTTP {resp.status}")
        http_status = resp.status

        for raw_line in resp:
            line = raw_line.decode("utf-8").strip()
            if not line.startswith("data: "):
                continue
            data_str = line[6:]
            if data_str == "[DONE]":
                break
            try:
                chunk = json.loads(data_str)
                timings = chunk.get("timings")
                if isinstance(timings, dict):
                    if "prompt_ms" in timings:
                        prompt_ms = float(timings["prompt_ms"])
                        has_server_timings = True
                    if "predicted_ms" in timings:
                        predicted_ms = float(timings["predicted_ms"])
                        has_server_timings = True
                    if "cache_n" in timings and int(timings["cache_n"]) >= 0:
                        cached_tokens = int(timings["cache_n"])
                    if "prompt_n" in timings and int(timings["prompt_n"]) >= 0:
                        uncached_prompt_tokens = int(timings["prompt_n"])
                    if "predicted_n" in timings and int(timings["predicted_n"]) > 0:
                        completion_tokens = int(timings["predicted_n"])

                usage = chunk.get("usage")
                if isinstance(usage, dict):
                    if "prompt_tokens" in usage and int(usage["prompt_tokens"]) > 0:
                        usage_prompt_tokens = int(usage["prompt_tokens"])
                    if "completion_tokens" in usage and int(usage["completion_tokens"]) > 0:
                        completion_tokens = int(usage["completion_tokens"])

                choices = chunk.get("choices")
                if choices and len(choices) > 0:
                    c0 = choices[0]
                    fr = c0.get("finish_reason")
                    if fr:
                        finish_reason = str(fr)
                    delta = c0.get("delta", {})
                    has_data = False
                    content = delta.get("content")
                    if content:
                        has_data = True
                        token_count += 1
                        out_text.append(content)
                    tool_calls = delta.get("tool_calls")
                    if tool_calls:
                        has_data = True
                        token_count += len(tool_calls)
                    if has_data and first_chunk:
                        ttft_ms = (time.monotonic() - t0) * 1000.0
                        first_chunk = False
            except Exception:
                pass

    prompt_tokens = usage_prompt_tokens if usage_prompt_tokens > 0 else (cached_tokens + uncached_prompt_tokens)
    if has_server_timings:
        total_neural_ms = prompt_ms + predicted_ms
    else:
        total_neural_ms = (time.monotonic() - t0) * 1000.0
        prompt_ms = ttft_ms
        predicted_ms = max(0.0, total_neural_ms - ttft_ms)

    if completion_tokens == 0:
        completion_tokens = token_count
    if not finish_reason:
        finish_reason = "stop"

    itl_ms = (total_neural_ms - ttft_ms) / float(completion_tokens - 1) if completion_tokens > 1 else 0.0
    return total_neural_ms, prompt_ms, predicted_ms, ttft_ms, itl_ms, prompt_tokens, completion_tokens, cached_tokens, uncached_prompt_tokens, finish_reason, http_status, "".join(out_text)


def run_workload(tier: str, warmth: str, rss_idle: float) -> dict:
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    t_neural_total = 0.0
    total_prompt_ms = 0.0
    total_predicted_ms = 0.0
    ttft_first = 0.0
    itl_avg = 0.0
    t_dispatch_total = 0.0
    t_prune_total = 0.0
    total_prompt_tokens = 0
    total_completion_tokens = 0
    total_cached_tokens = 0
    total_uncached_prompt_tokens = 0
    tool_calls = 0
    last_finish_reason = ""
    last_http_status = 200

    is_tool_rag = (
        os.environ.get("OPENCLAW_MODE") == "tool_rag"
        or os.environ.get("PICOCLAW_MODE") == "tool_rag"
        or os.environ.get("TOOL_RAG_MODE") == "true"
    )

    t_cat0 = time.monotonic()
    if is_tool_rag:
        full_catalog = build_tool_catalog(54)
        tool_emb = build_tool_embeddings(54)
        tool_catalog, tp_ms = prune_tools_vector_rag(full_catalog, tool_emb, tier, 5)
        t_prune_total += tp_ms
    else:
        full_catalog = []
        tool_emb = np.empty((0, 384), dtype=np.float32)
        tool_catalog = build_tool_catalog(15)
    t_cat_ms = (time.monotonic() - t_cat0) * 1000.0

    mock_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "mock_files")
    log_file = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "system_logs.jsonl")

    if tier == "W1":
        t_dispatch_total = t_cat_ms
        prompt = (
            "Analyze Apple Silicon M4 Unified Memory Architecture cache coherence and LPDDR5X bandwidth allocation. " * 8
            + "Summarize the primary latency determinant in two concise sentences."
        )
        msgs = [
            {"role": "system", "content": "You are a concise technical systems assistant."},
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt},
        ]
        tn, p_ms, pred_ms, ttft, itl, ptok, ctok, c_tok, unc_tok, fr, hstatus, _ = call_llama_stream(msgs, tool_catalog, 64)
        t_neural_total = tn
        total_prompt_ms = p_ms
        total_predicted_ms = pred_ms
        ttft_first = ttft
        itl_avg = itl
        total_prompt_tokens = ptok
        total_completion_tokens = ctok
        total_cached_tokens = c_tok
        total_uncached_prompt_tokens = unc_tok
        last_finish_reason = fr
        last_http_status = hstatus
        tool_calls = 0

    elif tier == "W2":
        t0_tool = time.monotonic()
        res1 = execute_tool_list_dir(mock_dir)
        vals, res2 = execute_tool_grep_logs(log_file)
        res3 = execute_tool_calc_stats(vals)
        t_dispatch_total = t_cat_ms + (time.monotonic() - t0_tool) * 1000.0
        tool_calls = 3

        prompt = f"System Tool Chain Results:\n1. list_dir: {res1}\n2. grep_logs: {res2}\n3. calc_stats: {res3}\nEvaluate the bus latency metrics and execute the next diagnostic step."
        msgs = [
            {"role": "system", "content": "You are a concise technical systems assistant."},
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt},
        ]
        tn, p_ms, pred_ms, ttft, itl, ptok, ctok, c_tok, unc_tok, fr, hstatus, _ = call_llama_stream(msgs, tool_catalog, 128)
        t_neural_total = tn
        total_prompt_ms = p_ms
        total_predicted_ms = pred_ms
        ttft_first = ttft
        itl_avg = itl
        total_prompt_tokens = ptok
        total_completion_tokens = ctok
        total_cached_tokens = c_tok
        total_uncached_prompt_tokens = unc_tok
        last_finish_reason = fr
        last_http_status = hstatus

    elif tier == "W3":
        t0_tool = time.monotonic()
        rag_context = execute_vector_rag(BASE_DIR)
        t_dispatch_total = t_cat_ms + (time.monotonic() - t0_tool) * 1000.0
        tool_calls = 1

        msgs = [
            {"role": "system", "content": "You are a concise technical systems assistant."},
            {"role": "user", "content": "Retrieved Context:\n" + rag_context + "\nQuestion: How do UMA bandwidth and runtime GC interact during local agent execution?"},
        ]
        tn, p_ms, pred_ms, ttft, itl, ptok, ctok, c_tok, unc_tok, fr, hstatus, _ = call_llama_stream(msgs, tool_catalog, 256)
        t_neural_total = tn
        total_prompt_ms = p_ms
        total_predicted_ms = pred_ms
        ttft_first = ttft
        itl_avg = itl
        total_prompt_tokens = ptok
        total_completion_tokens = ctok
        total_cached_tokens = c_tok
        total_uncached_prompt_tokens = unc_tok
        last_finish_reason = fr
        last_http_status = hstatus

    elif tier == "W4":
        tool_calls = 10
        itl_sum = 0.0
        history = [
            {"role": "system", "content": "You are a concise technical systems assistant."},
        ]
        t_dispatch_total = t_cat_ms
        for step in range(1, 11):
            t0_tool = time.monotonic()
            if is_tool_rag and step > 1:
                tool_catalog, tp_ms = prune_tools_vector_rag(full_catalog, tool_emb, tier, 5)
                t_prune_total += tp_ms
            execute_tool_list_dir(mock_dir)
            vals, _ = execute_tool_grep_logs(log_file)
            stat_str = execute_tool_calc_stats(vals)
            t_dispatch_total += (time.monotonic() - t0_tool) * 1000.0

            history.append({
                "role": "user",
                "content": f"Turn {step} Observation: {stat_str}. State next brief diagnostic action.",
            })
            tn, p_ms, pred_ms, ttft, itl, ptok, ctok, c_tok, unc_tok, fr, hstatus, reply = call_llama_stream(history, tool_catalog, 64)
            t_neural_total += tn
            total_prompt_ms += p_ms
            total_predicted_ms += pred_ms
            if step == 1:
                ttft_first = ttft
            itl_sum += itl
            total_prompt_tokens += ptok
            total_completion_tokens += ctok
            total_cached_tokens += c_tok
            total_uncached_prompt_tokens += unc_tok
            last_finish_reason = fr
            last_http_status = hstatus
            history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": reply})
        itl_avg = itl_sum / 10.0

    if total_prompt_tokens <= 0 or total_completion_tokens <= 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"FATAL: Validation failed - prompt_tokens ({total_prompt_tokens}) <= 0 or completion_tokens ({total_completion_tokens}) <= 0")

    t_task_ms = (time.monotonic() - t_start) * 1000.0
    t_http_ms = max(0.0, t_task_ms - t_neural_total - t_dispatch_total)
    t_orch_ms = max(0.0, t_task_ms - t_neural_total)
    phi_orch = (t_orch_ms / t_task_ms * 100.0) if t_task_ms > 0 else 0.0
    r_tool = (tool_calls / (t_dispatch_total / 1000.0)) if (tool_calls > 0 and t_dispatch_total > 0) else 0.0
    rss_peak = max(rss_idle, get_rss_mb())
    alpha_mem = rss_peak / max(1.0, rss_idle)
    cache_hit_pct = (total_cached_tokens / total_prompt_tokens * 100.0) if total_prompt_tokens > 0 else 0.0

    return {
        "architecture": "PythonNative (CPython 3.12)",
        "workload": tier,
        "warmth": warmth,
        "t_task_ms": t_task_ms,
        "t_neural_ms": t_neural_total,
        "prompt_ms": total_prompt_ms,
        "predicted_ms": total_predicted_ms,
        "t_orch_ms": t_orch_ms,
        "phi_orch_pct": phi_orch,
        "ttft_ms": ttft_first,
        "itl_ms": itl_avg,
        "t_dispatch_ms": t_dispatch_total,
        "t_prune_ms": t_prune_total,
        "t_http_ms": t_http_ms,
        "r_tool_ops_sec": r_tool,
        "rss_idle_mb": rss_idle,
        "rss_peak_mb": rss_peak,
        "alpha_mem": alpha_mem,
        "prompt_tokens": total_prompt_tokens,
        "completion_tokens": total_completion_tokens,
        "output_tokens": total_completion_tokens,
        "cached_tokens": total_cached_tokens,
        "uncached_prompt_tokens": total_uncached_prompt_tokens,
        "cache_hit_pct": cache_hit_pct,
        "tool_calls": tool_calls,
        "http_status": last_http_status,
        "finish_reason": last_finish_reason,
    }


def run_local_tools_benchmark(tier: str, iterations: int) -> dict:
    mock_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "mock_files")
    log_file = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "system_logs.jsonl")
    total_ops = 0
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if tier == "W2":
        for _ in range(iterations):
            build_tool_catalog(15)
            execute_tool_list_dir(mock_dir)
            vals, _ = execute_tool_grep_logs(log_file)
            execute_tool_calc_stats(vals)
            total_ops += 3
    elif tier == "W3":
        for _ in range(iterations):
            build_tool_catalog(15)
            execute_vector_rag(BASE_DIR)
            total_ops += 1
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported tier for local tools: {tier}")
    elapsed_ms = (time.monotonic() - t0) * 1000.0
    return {
        "workload": tier,
        "iterations": iterations,
        "total_ops": total_ops,
        "t_exec_ms": round(elapsed_ms, 3),
    }


def main():
    if len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] in ("--version", "version", "--help"):
        print("python_orch v1.0.0")
        sys.exit(0)
    if len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] == "--local-tools":
        tier = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "W2"
        iterations = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 200
        res = run_local_tools_benchmark(tier, iterations)
        print(json.dumps([res]))
        return
    tier = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "W1"
    warmth = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "Cold"
    reps = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 1
    rss_idle = get_rss_mb()
    results = []
    for _ in range(reps):
        res = run_workload(tier, warmth, rss_idle)
        results.append(res)
    print(json.dumps(results))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
benchmark/telemetry.py - Hardware Power & Process Resource Telemetry
Implements strict empirical telemetry parsers, numerical energy integrators (trapezoidal rule),
Apple Silicon SMC hardware shunt monitors (powermetrics), Linux RAPL energy meters,
and Darwin XNU libproc per-process micro-architectural resource monitors.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import ctypes
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
from typing import Any

# Darwin XNU libproc bindings for RUSAGE_INFO_V4 hardware polling
if sys.platform == "darwin":
    try:
        _libproc = ctypes.CDLL("/usr/lib/libproc.dylib")
    except Exception:
        _libproc = None
else:
    _libproc = None


class _rusage_info_v4(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [
        ("ri_uuid", ctypes.c_uint8 * 16),
        ("ri_user_time", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_system_time", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_pkg_idle_wkups", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_interrupt_wkups", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_pageins", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_wired_size", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_resident_size", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_phys_footprint", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_proc_start_abstime", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_proc_exit_abstime", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_child_user_time", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_child_system_time", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_child_pkg_idle_wkups", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_child_interrupt_wkups", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_child_pageins", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_child_elapsed_abstime", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_diskio_bytesread", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_diskio_byteswritten", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_cpu_time_qos_default", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_cpu_time_qos_maintenance", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_cpu_time_qos_background", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_cpu_time_qos_utility", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_cpu_time_qos_legacy", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_cpu_time_qos_user_initiated", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_cpu_time_qos_user_interactive", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_billed_system_time", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_serviced_system_time", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_logical_writes", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_lifetime_max_phys_footprint", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_instructions", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_cycles", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_billed_energy", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_serviced_energy", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_interval_max_phys_footprint", ctypes.c_uint64),
        ("ri_runnable_time", ctypes.c_uint64),
    ]


def get_process_rusage_v4(pid: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """
    Extracts POSIX process hardware counter metrics via Darwin XNU libproc.proc_pid_rusage.
    Returns resident set size, lifetime peak physical footprint, instructions, cycles, and billed energy.
    Falls back to psutil on Linux, or returns None if unavailable.
    """
    if pid is None:
        pid = os.getpid()
    if sys.platform == "darwin" and _libproc is not None:
        try:
            info = _rusage_info_v4()
            res = _libproc.proc_pid_rusage(ctypes.c_int(pid), ctypes.c_int(4), ctypes.byref(info))
            if res == 0:
                return {
                    "proc_rss_mb": round(info.ri_resident_size / (1024.0 * 1024.0), 2),
                    "proc_peak_phys_mb": round(info.ri_lifetime_max_phys_footprint / (1024.0 * 1024.0), 2),
                    "proc_instructions": int(info.ri_instructions),
                    "proc_cycles": int(info.ri_cycles),
                    "proc_billed_energy": int(info.ri_billed_energy),
                }
        except Exception:
            return None

    # Generic POSIX / Linux psutil fallback
    try:
        import psutil
        p = psutil.Process(pid)
        mem = p.memory_info()
        return {
            "proc_rss_mb": round(mem.rss / (1024.0 * 1024.0), 2),
            "proc_peak_phys_mb": round(mem.rss / (1024.0 * 1024.0), 2),
            "proc_instructions": 0,
            "proc_cycles": 0,
            "proc_billed_energy": 0,
        }
    except Exception:
        return None


def run_subprocess_with_rusage(
    cmd: list[str],
    cwd: str | None = None,
    env: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    timeout: float | None = None,
) -> tuple[str, str, int, dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Executes a child process via subprocess.Popen and monitors hardware performance
    counters via Darwin XNU libproc (RUSAGE_INFO_V4) or Linux psutil.
    Returns (stdout, stderr, returncode, hardware_metrics_dict).
    """
    proc = subprocess.Popen(
        cmd,
        cwd=cwd,
        env=env,
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
        stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
        text=True,
    )
    hw_stats = {
        "proc_instructions": 0,
        "proc_cycles": 0,
        "proc_billed_energy": 0,
        "proc_peak_phys_mb": 0.0,
        "proc_rss_mb": 0.0,
    }
    stop_event = threading.Event()

    def sampler():
        while not stop_event.is_set():
            if proc.poll() is not None:
                break
            st = get_process_rusage_v4(proc.pid)
            if st:
                for k in hw_stats:
                    if k in st and st[k] > hw_stats[k]:
                        hw_stats[k] = st[k]
            time.sleep(0.002)

    th = threading.Thread(target=sampler, daemon=True)
    th.start()
    try:
        stdout, stderr = proc.communicate(timeout=timeout)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        proc.kill()
        stdout, stderr = proc.communicate()
        raise
    finally:
        stop_event.set()
        th.join(timeout=0.1)

    return stdout, stderr, proc.returncode, hw_stats


def parse_powermetrics_sample(sample_text: str, default_timestamp_ns: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Parses a single Apple SMC powermetrics text block.
    Extracts CPU Power, GPU Power, ANE Power, Combined Power, and elapsed timing.
    """
    cpu_mw = 0.0
    gpu_mw = 0.0
    ane_mw = 0.0
    combined_mw = 0.0

    m_cpu = re.search(r"CPU Power:\s*([\d\.]+)\s*mW", sample_text)
    if m_cpu:
        cpu_mw = float(m_cpu.group(1))

    m_gpu = re.search(r"GPU Power:\s*([\d\.]+)\s*mW", sample_text)
    if m_gpu:
        gpu_mw = float(m_gpu.group(1))

    m_ane = re.search(r"ANE Power:\s*([\d\.]+)\s*mW", sample_text)
    if m_ane:
        ane_mw = float(m_ane.group(1))

    m_comb = re.search(r"Combined Power \(CPU \+ GPU \+ ANE\):\s*([\d\.]+)\s*mW", sample_text)
    if m_comb:
        combined_mw = float(m_comb.group(1))
    else:
        combined_mw = cpu_mw + gpu_mw + ane_mw

    # Parse elapsed duration if present
    m_elapsed = re.search(r"\(([\d\.]+)ms elapsed\)", sample_text)
    elapsed_ms = float(m_elapsed.group(1)) if m_elapsed else 50.0

    ts_ns = default_timestamp_ns if default_timestamp_ns is not None else time.monotonic_ns()

    return {
        "timestamp_ns": ts_ns,
        "elapsed_ms": elapsed_ms,
        "cpu_mw": cpu_mw,
        "gpu_mw": gpu_mw,
        "ane_mw": ane_mw,
        "package_mw": combined_mw,
    }


def integrate_energy_trapezoidal(samples: list[dict[str, Any]], idle_package_mw: float = 0.0) -> dict[str, float]:
    """
    Numerically integrates power samples over time using the composite trapezoidal rule:
        Energy (J) = sum_{i=0}^{N-2} ((P_i + P_{i+1}) / 2000.0) * dt_s
    Accounts for real non-uniform monotonic timestamps (timestamp_ns).
    Computes separate electrical energy rails for CPU, GPU, Apple Neural Engine, Total SoC Package,
    and Net energy above quiescent idle baseline.
    """
    if not samples:
        return {
            "energy_cpu_j": 0.0,
            "energy_gpu_j": 0.0,
            "energy_ane_j": 0.0,
            "energy_total_j": 0.0,
            "energy_net_above_idle_j": 0.0,
            "duration_s": 0.0,
            "avg_power_w": 0.0,
        }

    if len(samples) == 1:
        s0 = samples[0]
        dt = s0.get("elapsed_ms", 50.0) / 1000.0
        e_cpu = (s0["cpu_mw"] / 1000.0) * dt
        e_gpu = (s0["gpu_mw"] / 1000.0) * dt
        e_ane = (s0["ane_mw"] / 1000.0) * dt
        e_total = (s0["package_mw"] / 1000.0) * dt
        e_net = max(0.0, e_total - (idle_package_mw / 1000.0) * dt)
        return {
            "energy_cpu_j": round(e_cpu, 4),
            "energy_gpu_j": round(e_gpu, 4),
            "energy_ane_j": round(e_ane, 4),
            "energy_total_j": round(e_total, 4),
            "energy_net_above_idle_j": round(e_net, 4),
            "duration_s": round(dt, 4),
            "avg_power_w": round(e_total / dt, 4) if dt > 0 else 0.0,
        }

    e_cpu = 0.0
    e_gpu = 0.0
    e_ane = 0.0
    e_total = 0.0
    t_start = samples[0]["timestamp_ns"]
    t_end = samples[-1]["timestamp_ns"]
    duration_s = max((t_end - t_start) / 1e9, 1e-6)

    for i in range(len(samples) - 1):
        s0 = samples[i]
        s1 = samples[i + 1]
        dt = (s1["timestamp_ns"] - s0["timestamp_ns"]) / 1e9
        if dt <= 0:
            dt = s1.get("elapsed_ms", 50.0) / 1000.0

        e_cpu += ((s0["cpu_mw"] + s1["cpu_mw"]) / 2000.0) * dt
        e_gpu += ((s0["gpu_mw"] + s1["gpu_mw"]) / 2000.0) * dt
        e_ane += ((s0["ane_mw"] + s1["ane_mw"]) / 2000.0) * dt
        e_total += ((s0["package_mw"] + s1["package_mw"]) / 2000.0) * dt

    e_idle = (idle_package_mw / 1000.0) * duration_s
    e_net = max(0.0, e_total - e_idle)
    avg_power_w = (e_total / duration_s) if duration_s > 0 else 0.0

    return {
        "energy_cpu_j": round(e_cpu, 4),
        "energy_gpu_j": round(e_gpu, 4),
        "energy_ane_j": round(e_ane, 4),
        "energy_total_j": round(e_total, 4),
        "energy_net_above_idle_j": round(e_net, 4),
        "duration_s": round(duration_s, 4),
        "avg_power_w": round(avg_power_w, 4),
    }


class SMCPowerMonitor:
    """
    Apple SMC Hardware Power Rail Monitor using sudo powermetrics.
    Maintains a single continuous powermetrics background process across the campaign
    sampling at 50 ms resolution. Slices energy integration across trial time windows
    [t_start_ns, t_end_ns] without restarting powermetrics between trials.
    If non-root and sudo is unavailable or on non-macOS platforms,
    degrades gracefully without synthetic estimation, returning None for energy metrics.
    """

    def __init__(self, sample_interval_ms: int = 50):
        self.sample_interval_ms = sample_interval_ms
        self.is_macos = sys.platform == "darwin"
        self.is_root = os.geteuid() == 0 if hasattr(os, "geteuid") else False
        self.has_powermetrics = bool(shutil.which("powermetrics"))
        self.active = self.is_macos and (self.is_root or self._check_sudo()) and self.has_powermetrics
        self.telemetry_backend = "apple_smc_powermetrics" if self.active else "unprivileged_no_smc"
        self.idle_package_mw = 0.0
        self.idle_cpu_mw = 0.0
        self.idle_gpu_mw = 0.0

        self._proc: subprocess.Popen | None = None
        self._thread: threading.Thread | None = None
        self._stop_event = threading.Event()
        self._samples: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._current_start_ns: int = 0

    def _check_sudo(self) -> bool:
        """Check if sudo is available without password prompt."""
        try:
            res = subprocess.run(["sudo", "-n", "true"], capture_output=True, timeout=1.0)
            return res.returncode == 0
        except Exception:
            return False

    def measure_idle_baseline(self, duration_sec: float = 5.0) -> float:
        """
        Measures quiescent idle baseline power dissipation for duration_sec seconds.
        Returns average idle package milliwatts.
        """
        if not self.active:
            self.idle_package_mw = 0.0
            return 0.0

        sample_count = max(2, int((duration_sec * 1000.0) / self.sample_interval_ms))
        cmd = [
            "sudo",
            "-n",
            "powermetrics",
            "-i",
            str(self.sample_interval_ms),
            "-n",
            str(sample_count),
            "--samplers",
            "cpu_power,gpu_power,ane_power",
        ]
        if self.is_root:
            cmd = cmd[2:]  # Remove 'sudo', '-n'
        try:
            res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=duration_sec + 5.0)
            blocks = res.stdout.split("*** Sampled system activity")
            samples = []
            for b in blocks:
                if "CPU Power" in b or "GPU Power" in b:
                    samples.append(parse_powermetrics_sample(b))
            if samples:
                self.idle_package_mw = sum(s["package_mw"] for s in samples) / len(samples)
                self.idle_cpu_mw = sum(s["cpu_mw"] for s in samples) / len(samples)
                self.idle_gpu_mw = sum(s["gpu_mw"] for s in samples) / len(samples)
            else:
                self.idle_package_mw = 0.0
                self.idle_cpu_mw = 0.0
                self.idle_gpu_mw = 0.0
        except Exception:
            self.idle_package_mw = 0.0
            self.idle_cpu_mw = 0.0
            self.idle_gpu_mw = 0.0

        return self.idle_package_mw

    def start_campaign(self) -> None:
        """Starts a SINGLE continuous background powermetrics sampling process for the entire campaign."""
        if not self.active or self._proc is not None:
            return

        with self._lock:
            self._samples = []
        self._stop_event.clear()

        cmd = [
            "sudo",
            "-n",
            "powermetrics",
            "-i",
            str(self.sample_interval_ms),
            "--samplers",
            "cpu_power,gpu_power,ane_power",
        ]
        if self.is_root:
            cmd = cmd[2:]

        try:
            self._proc = subprocess.Popen(
                cmd,
                stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                text=True,
                bufsize=1,
                cwd=None,
            )
            self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._reader_loop, daemon=True)
            self._thread.start()
        except Exception:
            self.active = False
            self.telemetry_backend = "unprivileged_no_smc"

    def stop_campaign(self) -> None:
        """Terminates the continuous powermetrics background sampling process."""
        if self._proc is None:
            return
        self._stop_event.set()
        try:
            self._proc.terminate()
            self._proc.wait(timeout=2.0)
        except Exception:
            try:
                self._proc.kill()
            except Exception:
                pass
        finally:
            self._proc = None

        if self._thread is not None:
            self._thread.join(timeout=1.0)
            self._thread = None

    def start(self, agent_key: str = "picoclaw") -> None:
        """Trial-level start: ensures campaign process is active and marks trial start timestamp."""
        if not self.active:
            return
        if self._proc is None:
            self.start_campaign()
        self._current_start_ns = time.monotonic_ns()

    def stop(self, agent_key: str | None = None) -> dict[str, float] | None:
        """Trial-level stop: slices energy from trial start timestamp to current time."""
        if not self.active:
            return None
        t_end_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
        return self.slice_energy(self._current_start_ns, t_end_ns)

    def slice_energy(self, t_start_ns: int, t_end_ns: int) -> dict[str, float] | None:
        """
        Integrates exactly the power samples contained within [t_start_ns, t_end_ns].
        Thread-safe slicing from continuous memory buffer.
        Note: The powermetrics sampling period is 50 ms (-i 50), introducing a synchronization
        latency bounded by <= 50 ms.
        """
        if not self.active:
            return None

        # Short sleep to allow reader loop to buffer trailing sample
        time.sleep(0.06)

        with self._lock:
            if not self._samples:
                return None
            sub_samples = [s for s in self._samples if t_start_ns <= s["timestamp_ns"] <= t_end_ns]
            if not sub_samples:
                before = [s for s in self._samples if s["timestamp_ns"] < t_start_ns]
                after = [s for s in self._samples if s["timestamp_ns"] > t_end_ns]
                candidate = []
                if before:
                    candidate.append(before[-1])
                if after:
                    candidate.append(after[0])
                sub_samples = candidate

        if not sub_samples:
            return None

        if len(sub_samples) == 1:
            sub_samples = [dict(sub_samples[0], elapsed_ms=max(1.0, (t_end_ns - t_start_ns) / 1e6))]

        return integrate_energy_trapezoidal(sub_samples, self.idle_package_mw)

    def _reader_loop(self) -> None:
        if self._proc is None or self._proc.stdout is None:
            return
        buffer = []
        for line in self._proc.stdout:
            if self._stop_event.is_set():
                break
            if "*** Sampled system activity" in line:
                if buffer:
                    block = "".join(buffer)
                    s = parse_powermetrics_sample(block, time.monotonic_ns())
                    with self._lock:
                        self._samples.append(s)
                        if len(self._samples) > 10000:
                            self._samples = self._samples[-5000:]
                    buffer = [line]
                else:
                    buffer.append(line)
            else:
                buffer.append(line)


class LinuxRAPLMonitor:
    """
    Linux Running Average Power Limit (RAPL) sysfs hardware energy counter.
    Reads /sys/class/powercap/intel-rapl/intel-rapl:0/energy_uj before and after trial.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.is_linux = sys.platform.startswith("linux")
        self.rapl_path = "/sys/class/powercap/intel-rapl/intel-rapl:0/energy_uj"
        self.active = self.is_linux and os.path.exists(self.rapl_path) and os.access(self.rapl_path, os.R_OK)
        self.telemetry_backend = "linux_rapl" if self.active else "linux_psutil_unprivileged"
        self._start_uj: int | None = None
        self._start_time: float | None = None

    def start(self) -> None:
        if not self.active:
            return
        try:
            with open(self.rapl_path, "r") as f:
                self._start_uj = int(f.read().strip())
            self._start_time = time.monotonic()
        except Exception:
            self._start_uj = None

    def stop(self) -> dict[str, float] | None:
        if not self.active or self._start_uj is None or self._start_time is None:
            return None
        try:
            with open(self.rapl_path, "r") as f:
                end_uj = int(f.read().strip())
            end_time = time.monotonic()
            duration_s = max(end_time - self._start_time, 1e-6)
            diff_uj = end_uj - self._start_uj
            if diff_uj < 0:
                # Counter wrapped around 32-bit/64-bit threshold
                diff_uj += 2**32
            energy_j = diff_uj / 1_000_000.0
            return {
                "energy_cpu_j": round(energy_j, 4),
                "energy_gpu_j": 0.0,
                "energy_ane_j": 0.0,
                "energy_total_j": round(energy_j, 4),
                "energy_net_above_idle_j": round(energy_j, 4),
                "duration_s": round(duration_s, 4),
                "avg_power_w": round(energy_j / duration_s, 4) if duration_s > 0 else 0.0,
            }
        except Exception:
            return None

================================================================================
FILE: benchmark/adapters/base.py
SHA-256: 65a4f485692ead175d82a48631f69051ed2caff44fa844a7c871db0ed4092098

"""
Base Abstract Adapter for AI Agent Orchestrators
Interface Base Abstrata para Adaptadores de Orquestradores de Agentes de IA

Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
Language / Idioma: Python 3.12+ (English source code, bilingual docstrings)
"""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import Any, Dict, List


class AgentOrchestratorAdapter(ABC):
    """
    Abstract Base Class defining the contract for benchmarking AI agent orchestrators.
    Classe Base Abstrata que define o contrato para benchmark de orquestradores de agentes de IA.
    """

    def __init__(self, name: str, runtime: str, language: str, mode: str = "standard"):
        self.name = name
        self.runtime = runtime
        self.language = language
        self.mode = mode

    @abstractmethod
    def setup(self) -> bool:
        """
        Verify prerequisites, compile binaries or install dependencies.
        Verifica pré-requisitos, compila binários ou instala dependências.
        """
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def cold_start_cmd(self) -> List[str]:
        """
        Return the CLI command used for cold-start latency measurement via hyperfine.
        Retorna o comando CLI usado para medição de cold-start via hyperfine.
        """
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def run_workload(self, workload_id: str, prompt: str = "", iterations: int = 1) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Execute a standardized workload (W1-W4) and return structured telemetry.
        Executa uma carga de trabalho padronizada (W1-W4) e retorna telemetria estruturada.
        """
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def teardown(self) -> None:
        """
        Cleanup temporary session files, caches, or background processes.
        Limpa arquivos temporários de sessão, caches ou processos em segundo plano.
        """
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def metadata(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Return architectural and environment metadata for provenance tracking.
        Retorna metadados arquiteturais e de ambiente para rastreabilidade de proveniência.
        """
        pass

END FILE: benchmark/adapters/base.py

================================================================================
FILE: benchmark/adapters/picoclaw_adapter.py
SHA-256: 750f4f4b67d84006ecbdd22ac8208ae3f891301d8d895cc0a0c8adb397e77975

"""
PicoClaw Orchestrator Adapter (Go 1.23.2 Mach-O ARM64)
Adaptador do Orquestrador PicoClaw (Go 1.23.2 Mach-O ARM64)

Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
"""

import json
import os
import subprocess
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from benchmark.adapters.base import AgentOrchestratorAdapter
from benchmark.telemetry import run_subprocess_with_rusage


class PicoClawAdapter(AgentOrchestratorAdapter):
    """
    Adapter for Sipeed PicoClaw compiled native Mach-O ARM64 Go binary.
    Adaptador para o binário nativo Go Mach-O ARM64 compilado do Sipeed PicoClaw.
    """

    def __init__(self, repo_dir: str, mode: str = "native_static"):
        super().__init__(
            name="PicoClaw" if mode != "tool_rag" else "PicoClawToolRAG",
            runtime="Go 1.23.2 (Native Mach-O ARM64)",
            language="Go",
            mode=mode
        )
        self.repo_dir = repo_dir
        self.bin_path = os.path.join(repo_dir, "benchmark", "picoclaw_orch")
        self.cli_path = os.path.join(repo_dir, "tools", "picoclaw", "picoclaw")

    def setup(self) -> bool:
        if not os.path.exists(self.bin_path):
            go_src = os.path.join(self.repo_dir, "benchmark", "picoclaw_orch.go")
            go_bin = os.path.join(self.repo_dir, "tools", "go", "bin", "go")
            go_cmd = go_bin if os.path.exists(go_bin) else "go"
            if os.path.exists(go_src):
                subprocess.run(
                    [go_cmd, "build", "-o", self.bin_path, go_src],
                    cwd=os.path.join(self.repo_dir, "benchmark"),
                    check=True
                )
        return os.path.exists(self.bin_path)

    def cold_start_cmd(self) -> List[str]:
        if os.path.exists(self.cli_path):
            return [self.cli_path, "version"]
        return [self.bin_path, "version"]

    def run_workload(self, workload_id: str, prompt: str = "", iterations: int = 1) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        env = os.environ.copy()
        if self.mode == "tool_rag":
            env["PICOCLAW_MODE"] = "tool_rag"
            env["OPENCLAW_MODE"] = "tool_rag"
        cmd = [self.bin_path, workload_id]
        stdout, stderr, rc, hw_stats = run_subprocess_with_rusage(cmd, cwd=self.repo_dir, env=env)
        if rc != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"PicoClaw execution failed (code {rc}): {stderr}")
        raw_output = stdout.strip()
        data = json.loads(raw_output)
        if isinstance(data, list) and len(data) > 0:
            res = data[0]
        else:
            res = data
        res["proc_instructions"] = hw_stats.get("proc_instructions", 0)
        res["proc_cycles"] = hw_stats.get("proc_cycles", 0)
        res["proc_billed_energy"] = hw_stats.get("proc_billed_energy", 0)
        res["proc_peak_phys_mb"] = hw_stats.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", 0.0)
        if res.get("rss_peak_mb", 0.0) == 0.0 and hw_stats.get("proc_rss_mb", 0.0) > 0.0:
            res["rss_peak_mb"] = hw_stats["proc_rss_mb"]
        return res

    def teardown(self) -> None:
        pass

    def metadata(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "runtime": self.runtime,
            "language": self.language,
            "mode": self.mode,
            "binary_path": self.bin_path,
            "gc_architecture": "Non-generational tri-color concurrent mark-sweep",
            "concurrency_model": "M:N lightweight Goroutines (~2 KB initial stack)"
        }

================================================================================
END FILE: benchmark/adapters/picoclaw_adapter.py

"""
OpenClaw Orchestrator Adapter (Node.js v26 / TypeScript on Google V8)
Adaptador do Orquestrador OpenClaw (Node.js v26 / TypeScript sobre Google V8)

Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
"""

import json
import os
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from benchmark.adapters.base import AgentOrchestratorAdapter
from benchmark.telemetry import run_subprocess_with_rusage


class OpenClawAdapter(AgentOrchestratorAdapter):
    """
    Adapter for OpenClaw orchestrator executing atop Node.js V8.
    Supports ablation modes: 'minimal' (equalized 3 tools) and 'full' (54 tools stock enterprise).
    """

    def __init__(self, repo_dir: str, mode: str = "minimal"):
        super().__init__(
            name="OpenClaw",
            runtime="Node.js v26.12.1 (Google V8 JIT)",
            language="TypeScript / JavaScript",
            mode=mode
        )
        self.repo_dir = repo_dir
        self.script_path = os.path.join(repo_dir, "benchmark", "openclaw_orch.mjs")

        # Locate node binary
        embedded_node = os.path.join(repo_dir, "tools", "node", "bin", "node")
        if os.path.exists(embedded_node):
            self.node_bin = embedded_node
        else:
            self.node_bin = "node"

    def setup(self) -> bool:
        return os.path.exists(self.script_path) and (os.path.exists(self.node_bin) or self.node_bin == "node")

    def cold_start_cmd(self) -> List[str]:
        return [self.node_bin, self.script_path, "--version"]

    def run_workload(self, workload_id: str, prompt: str = "", iterations: int = 1) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        env = os.environ.copy()
        env["OPENCLAW_MODE"] = self.mode
        cmd = [self.node_bin, self.script_path, workload_id]
        stdout, stderr, rc, hw_stats = run_subprocess_with_rusage(cmd, cwd=self.repo_dir, env=env)
        if rc != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"OpenClaw execution failed (code {rc}): {stderr}")
        raw_output = stdout.strip()
        data = json.loads(raw_output)
        if isinstance(data, list) and len(data) > 0:
            res = data[0]
        else:
            res = data
        res["proc_instructions"] = hw_stats.get("proc_instructions", 0)
        res["proc_cycles"] = hw_stats.get("proc_cycles", 0)
        res["proc_billed_energy"] = hw_stats.get("proc_billed_energy", 0)
        res["proc_peak_phys_mb"] = hw_stats.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", 0.0)
        if res.get("rss_peak_mb", 0.0) == 0.0 and hw_stats.get("proc_rss_mb", 0.0) > 0.0:
            res["rss_peak_mb"] = hw_stats["proc_rss_mb"]
        return res

    def teardown(self) -> None:
        pass

    def metadata(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "runtime": self.runtime,
            "language": self.language,
            "mode": self.mode,
            "tool_catalog_size": 54 if self.mode == "full" else 15,
            "node_binary": self.node_bin,
            "gc_architecture": "Generational mark-compact (Scavenger + Old Space)",
            "schema_validator": "TypeBox (@sinclair/typebox JSON-Schema)"
        }

"""
Python Native Reference Orchestrator Adapter (CPython 3.12 VM)
Adaptador do Orquestrador de Referência Nativo em Python (CPython 3.12 VM)

Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import sys
from typing import Any

from benchmark.adapters.base import AgentOrchestratorAdapter
from benchmark.telemetry import run_subprocess_with_rusage


class PythonNativeAdapter(AgentOrchestratorAdapter):
    """
    Adapter for Python 3.12 reference agent orchestrator.
    Evaluates CPython VM interpretation, memory layout, and tool dispatch.
    """

    def __init__(self, repo_dir: str, mode: str = "python_stdlib"):
        super().__init__(
            name="PythonNative",
            runtime=f"Python {sys.version.split()[0]} (CPython VM)",
            language="Python",
            mode=mode,
        )
        self.repo_dir = repo_dir
        self.script_path = os.path.join(repo_dir, "benchmark", "python_orch.py")
        venv_py = os.path.join(repo_dir, "tools", ".venv", "bin", "python")
        if os.path.exists(venv_py):
            self.py_bin = venv_py
        else:
            self.py_bin = sys.executable

    def setup(self) -> bool:
        return os.path.exists(self.script_path) and os.path.exists(self.py_bin)

    def cold_start_cmd(self) -> list[str]:
        return [self.py_bin, "-c", "import sys; print(sys.version)"]

    def run_workload(self, workload_id: str, prompt: str = "", iterations: int = 1) -> dict[str, Any]:
        cmd = [self.py_bin, self.script_path, workload_id, "Warm", str(iterations)]
        stdout, stderr, rc, hw_stats = run_subprocess_with_rusage(cmd, cwd=self.repo_dir)
        if rc != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"PythonNative execution failed (code {rc}): {stderr}")
        raw_output = stdout.strip()
        data = json.loads(raw_output)
        if isinstance(data, list) and len(data) > 0:
            res = data[0]
        else:
            res = data
        res["proc_instructions"] = hw_stats.get("proc_instructions", 0)
        res["proc_cycles"] = hw_stats.get("proc_cycles", 0)
        res["proc_billed_energy"] = hw_stats.get("proc_billed_energy", 0)
        res["proc_peak_phys_mb"] = hw_stats.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", 0.0)
        if res.get("rss_peak_mb", 0.0) == 0.0 and hw_stats.get("proc_rss_mb", 0.0) > 0.0:
            res["rss_peak_mb"] = hw_stats["proc_rss_mb"]
        return res

    def teardown(self) -> None:
        pass

    def metadata(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "runtime": self.runtime,
            "language": self.language,
            "mode": self.mode,
            "tool_catalog_size": 15,
            "interpreter_path": self.py_bin,
            "gc_architecture": "Reference Counting + Cyclic Garbage Collector",
            "concurrency_model": "1:1 OS Threads (GIL bound)",
        }

================================================================================
FILE: benchmark/adapters/bun_minimal_adapter.py
SHA-256: 1619f81b887b7d5559ccacb1eab2cf6e92a8481cab0c7f9a9796d9f38bc1acad

"""
Bun / JavaScriptCore Orchestrator Adapter (Independent 4th Runtime Adapter)
Adaptador Independente Bun / JavaScriptCore executando benchmark/bun_jsc_agent.mjs

Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
"""

import json
import os
import shutil
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from benchmark.adapters.base import AgentOrchestratorAdapter
from benchmark.telemetry import run_subprocess_with_rusage


class BunJSCOrchestratorAdapter(AgentOrchestratorAdapter):
    """Independent 4th runtime adapter executing benchmark/bun_jsc_agent.mjs atop native Bun / JavaScriptCore."""

    def __init__(self, repo_dir: str):
        super().__init__(
            name="BunJSCOrchestrator",
            runtime="Bun v1.4.4 / JavaScriptCore (darwin-aarch64)",
            language="JavaScript (ES2024 / Bun JSC Native APIs)",
            mode="minimal",
        )
        self.repo_dir = repo_dir
        self.script_path = os.path.join(repo_dir, "benchmark", "bun_jsc_agent.mjs")
        local_bun = os.path.join(repo_dir, "tools", "bun", "bin", "bun")
        local_node = os.path.join(repo_dir, "tools", "node", "bin", "node")
        self.runner_bin = (
            local_bun
            if os.path.exists(local_bun)
            else (
                shutil.which("bun")
                or (local_node if os.path.exists(local_node) else "node")
            )
        )

    def setup(self) -> bool:
        return os.path.exists(self.script_path)

    def cold_start_cmd(self) -> List[str]:
        return [self.runner_bin, "run", self.script_path, "--version"]

    def teardown(self) -> None:
        pass

    def metadata(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "runtime": self.runtime,
            "language": self.language,
            "mode": self.mode,
            "worker_script": "benchmark/bun_jsc_agent.mjs",
            "runner_binary_used": os.path.basename(self.runner_bin),
            "is_fallback_node": "bun" not in os.path.basename(self.runner_bin),
        }

    def run_workload(
        self, workload_id: str, prompt: str = "", iterations: int = 1
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        runner_base = os.path.basename(self.runner_bin)
        is_bun = "bun" in runner_base
        cmd = [self.runner_bin]
        if is_bun:
            cmd.append("run")
        cmd.extend([self.script_path, workload_id, "Warm", str(iterations)])

        stdout, stderr, rc, hw = run_subprocess_with_rusage(
            cmd,
            cwd=self.repo_dir,
            env={**os.environ},
        )
        if rc != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"BunJSCOrchestrator execution failed (code {rc}): {stderr}")

        res = json.loads(stdout.strip())
        res = res[0] if isinstance(res, list) and res else res
        res.update(
            {
                k: hw.get(k, 0)
                for k in (
                    "proc_instructions",
                    "proc_cycles",
                    "proc_billed_energy",
                    "proc_peak_phys_mb",
                )
            }
        )
        res["runner_binary_used"] = runner_base
        res["is_fallback_node"] = not is_bun
        res["worker_script"] = "benchmark/bun_jsc_agent.mjs"
        return res


# Backward-compatible alias
BunMinimalAdapter = BunJSCOrchestratorAdapter

"""
Adapters Package Initialization and Factory Function
Inicialização do Pacote de Adaptadores e Função Factory

Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
"""
from typing import Callable, Dict

from benchmark.adapters.base import AgentOrchestratorAdapter
from benchmark.adapters.bun_minimal_adapter import (
    BunJSCOrchestratorAdapter,
    BunMinimalAdapter,
)
from benchmark.adapters.neural_baseline_adapter import NeuralBaselineAdapter
from benchmark.adapters.openclaw_adapter import OpenClawAdapter
from benchmark.adapters.picoclaw_adapter import PicoClawAdapter
from benchmark.adapters.python_native_adapter import PythonNativeAdapter

# Neutral Architecture-First Class Aliases
GoNativeOrchestratorAdapter = PicoClawAdapter
NodeV8OrchestratorAdapter = OpenClawAdapter
PythonReferenceAdapter = PythonNativeAdapter
LocalLLMBaselineAdapter = NeuralBaselineAdapter

ADAPTER_REGISTRY: Dict[str, Callable[[str], AgentOrchestratorAdapter]] = {
    # Canonical Neutral Architecture Keys
    "go_orchestrator": lambda repo_dir: GoNativeOrchestratorAdapter(repo_dir),
    "go_orchestrator_tool_rag": lambda repo_dir: GoNativeOrchestratorAdapter(repo_dir, mode="tool_rag"),
    "node_orchestrator": lambda repo_dir: NodeV8OrchestratorAdapter(repo_dir, mode="minimal"),
    "node_orchestrator_minimal": lambda repo_dir: NodeV8OrchestratorAdapter(repo_dir, mode="minimal"),
    "node_orchestrator_full": lambda repo_dir: NodeV8OrchestratorAdapter(repo_dir, mode="full"),
    "node_orchestrator_tool_rag": lambda repo_dir: NodeV8OrchestratorAdapter(repo_dir, mode="tool_rag"),
    "python_reference_agent": lambda repo_dir: PythonReferenceAdapter(repo_dir),
    "local_llm_baseline": lambda repo_dir: LocalLLMBaselineAdapter(),
    "bun_jsc_orchestrator": lambda repo_dir: BunJSCOrchestratorAdapter(repo_dir),
    # Compatible Product-Reference Aliases
    "picoclaw": lambda repo_dir: GoNativeOrchestratorAdapter(repo_dir),
    "picoclaw_tool_rag": lambda repo_dir: GoNativeOrchestratorAdapter(repo_dir, mode="tool_rag"),
    "openclaw_minimal": lambda repo_dir: NodeV8OrchestratorAdapter(repo_dir, mode="minimal"),
    "openclaw_full": lambda repo_dir: NodeV8OrchestratorAdapter(repo_dir, mode="full"),
    "openclaw_tool_rag": lambda repo_dir: NodeV8OrchestratorAdapter(repo_dir, mode="tool_rag"),
    "python_native": lambda repo_dir: PythonReferenceAdapter(repo_dir),
    "neural_baseline": lambda repo_dir: LocalLLMBaselineAdapter(),
    "bun_minimal": lambda repo_dir: BunJSCOrchestratorAdapter(repo_dir),
}


def get_adapter(name: str, repo_dir: str) -> AgentOrchestratorAdapter:
    """
    Instantiate and return an orchestrator adapter by registry key.
    Instancia e retorna um adaptador de orquestrador pela chave de registro.
    """
    key = name.lower()
    if key not in ADAPTER_REGISTRY:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown adapter '{name}'. Available: {list(ADAPTER_REGISTRY.keys())}")
    factory = ADAPTER_REGISTRY[key]
    return factory(repo_dir)


__all__ = [
    "AgentOrchestratorAdapter",
    "GoNativeOrchestratorAdapter",
    "NodeV8OrchestratorAdapter",
    "PythonReferenceAdapter",
    "LocalLLMBaselineAdapter",
    "BunJSCOrchestratorAdapter",
    "PicoClawAdapter",
    "OpenClawAdapter",
    "PythonNativeAdapter",
    "NeuralBaselineAdapter",
    "BunMinimalAdapter",
    "get_adapter",
    "ADAPTER_REGISTRY",
]
