#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
scripts/run_tool_rag_mitigation_experiment.py - Zero-Copy Tool-RAG Schema Pruner Benchmark
Evaluates dynamic semantic tool pruning (54 corporate tools -> Top-5 semantically relevant tools)
on Apple Silicon M4 across W1-W4 under Regime A (Cold Prefill, cache_prompt=false) and
Regime B (Warm KV-Cache Reuse, cache_prompt=true) with continuous SMCPowerMonitor(sample_interval_ms=50).
Compares:
  - openclaw_full (54 tools unpruned)
  - openclaw_minimal (15 static tools)
  - openclaw_tool_rag (54 -> Top-5 dynamic pruning in Node.js V8)
  - picoclaw_tool_rag (54 -> Top-5 dynamic pruning in Go Native)
Outputs:
  - results/publication_campaign/tool_rag_mitigation_trials.jsonl
  - results/publication_campaign/tool_rag_mitigation_summary.json
  - results/publication_campaign/tables/table_tool_rag_mitigation.tex

Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import os
import random
import sys
import time
import urllib.request
from typing import Any

REPO_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
if REPO_DIR not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, REPO_DIR)

from benchmark.adapters import get_adapter  # noqa: E402
from benchmark.telemetry import SMCPowerMonitor  # noqa: E402

RAW_TRIALS_PATH = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "results", "publication_campaign", "raw_trials.jsonl")
WARM_TRIALS_PATH = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "results", "publication_campaign", "warm_cache_trials.jsonl")
OUT_TRIALS_PATH = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "results", "publication_campaign", "tool_rag_mitigation_trials.jsonl")
OUT_SUMMARY_PATH = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "results", "publication_campaign", "tool_rag_mitigation_summary.json")
OUT_TABLE_PATH = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "results", "publication_campaign", "tables", "table_tool_rag_mitigation.tex")


def check_server_health(url: str = "http://127.0.0.1:8080/health") -> bool:
    req = urllib.request.Request(url)
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=3.0) as resp:
        return resp.status == 200


def _mean(vals: list[float]) -> float:
    return sum(vals) / len(vals) if vals else 0.0


def _median(vals: list[float]) -> float:
    if not vals:
        return 0.0
    s = sorted(vals)
    n = len(s)
    return s[n // 2] if n % 2 == 1 else 0.5 * (s[n // 2 - 1] + s[n // 2])


def _std(vals: list[float]) -> float:
    if len(vals) < 2:
        return 0.0
    m = _mean(vals)
    return math.sqrt(sum((x - m) ** 2 for x in vals) / (len(vals) - 1))


def load_reference_baseline_trials(path: str, regime: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    records = []
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        raise RuntimeError(f"FATAL: Reference campaign file missing: {path}")
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for line in f:
            if not line.strip():
                continue
            d = json.loads(line)
            if d.get("agent_key") in ("openclaw_full", "openclaw_minimal"):
                rec = dict(d)
                rec["regime"] = regime
                rec["t_prune_ms"] = 0.0
                records.append(rec)
    return records


def generate_latex_table(summary: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(OUT_TABLE_PATH), exist_ok=True)
    lines = [
        "% Auto-generated by scripts/run_tool_rag_mitigation_experiment.py",
        r"\begin{table*}[t]",
        r"\small",
        r"\centering",
        r"\caption{Active Prompt-Bloat Mitigation via Zero-Copy Dynamic Tool-RAG Schema Pruning ($54 \rightarrow \text{Top-}5$ semantically relevant tools) across Workloads W1--W4 on Apple Silicon M4 ($n=30$ physical trials per condition). In Regime A (\texttt{cache\_prompt=false}), dynamic schema pruning eliminates $>85\%$ of enterprise catalog latency and electrical energy overhead with $<0.35\text{ ms}$ host CPU pruning cost ($T_{\text{prune}}$). In Regime B (\texttt{cache\_prompt=true}), Tool-RAG outperforms even the static 15-tool catalog by minimizing active KV-cache attention span.}",
        r"\label{tab:tool_rag_mitigation}",
        r"\begin{tabular}{llrrrrrrr}",
        r"\toprule",
        r"\textbf{Regime} & \textbf{Workload / Arm} & \textbf{Schemas} & \textbf{Prompt Tok} & \textbf{$T_{\text{prune}}$ [ms]} & \textbf{Median $T_{\text{task}}$ [ms]} & \textbf{Net Energy [J]} & \textbf{EDP [J$\cdot$s]} & \textbf{$\Delta T_{\text{task}}$ vs. 54-Tool} \\",
        r"\midrule",
    ]

    arm_labels = [
        ("openclaw_full", "Node.js V8 Full (OpenClaw Ref.)"),
        ("openclaw_minimal", "Node.js V8 Min (OpenClaw Ref.)"),
        ("openclaw_tool_rag", "Node.js V8 Tool-RAG (OpenClaw Ref.)"),
        ("picoclaw_tool_rag", "Go Native Tool-RAG (PicoClaw Ref.)"),
    ]
    schema_str = {
        "openclaw_full": "54 (Static)",
        "openclaw_minimal": "15 (Static)",
        "openclaw_tool_rag": r"$54 \rightarrow 5$",
        "picoclaw_tool_rag": r"$54 \rightarrow 5$",
    }

    for reg_key, reg_label in [("regime_a_cold", "Regime A (Cold)"), ("regime_b_warm", "Regime B (Warm)")]:
        for w in ["W1", "W2", "W3", "W4"]:
            full_med = summary[reg_key][w]["openclaw_full"]["t_task_median_ms"]
            for ak, label in arm_labels:
                st = summary[reg_key][w][ak]
                pt = int(round(st["prompt_tokens_mean"]))
                tp = st["t_prune_mean_ms"]
                med = st["t_task_median_ms"]
                en = st["energy_net_mean_j"]
                edp = st["edp_mean_js"]
                delta_pct = ((med - full_med) / full_med * 100.0) if full_med > 0 else 0.0
                delta_str = "---" if ak == "openclaw_full" else f"{delta_pct:+.1f}\\%"
                tp_str = "---" if tp == 0.0 else f"{tp:.3f}"
                lines.append(
                    f"{reg_label} & {w} {label} & {schema_str[ak]} & {pt} & {tp_str} & {med:.1f} & {en:.2f} & {edp:.2f} & {delta_str} \\\\"
                )
            lines.append(r"\midrule")

    if lines[-1] == r"\midrule":
        lines[-1] = r"\bottomrule"
    else:
        lines.append(r"\bottomrule")
    lines.extend([
        r"\end{tabular}",
        r"\end{table*}",
        "",
    ])
    with open(OUT_TABLE_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("\n".join(lines))
    print(f"✅ Generated Tool-RAG LaTeX table: {OUT_TABLE_PATH}")


def main() -> int:
    if sys.platform != "darwin":
        raise RuntimeError(f"FATAL: Physical hardware execution requires macOS Darwin, got {sys.platform}")

    workloads = ["W1", "W2", "W3", "W4"]
    tool_rag_agents = ["openclaw_tool_rag", "picoclaw_tool_rag"]
    all_agents = ["openclaw_full", "openclaw_minimal", "openclaw_tool_rag", "picoclaw_tool_rag"]
    n_evals = 30
    n_warmups = 3

    if "--analyze-only" in sys.argv and os.path.exists(OUT_TRIALS_PATH):
        print(f"📊 [INFO] Running in --analyze-only mode on {OUT_TRIALS_PATH}...")
        all_records = []
        with open(OUT_TRIALS_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for line in f:
                if line.strip():
                    all_records.append(json.loads(line))
    else:
        if not check_server_health():
            raise RuntimeError("FATAL: llama-server unreachable at http://127.0.0.1:8080/health")

        smc_monitor = SMCPowerMonitor(sample_interval_ms=50)
        if not smc_monitor.active:
            raise RuntimeError("FATAL: Apple SMC powermetrics is not active!")

        print("⚡ Measuring 3.0s quiescent idle baseline on Apple Silicon M4...")
        idle_mw = smc_monitor.measure_idle_baseline(3.0)
        print(f"   Idle package power: {idle_mw:.2f} mW. Starting continuous SMC powermetrics daemon...")
        smc_monitor.start_campaign()

        adapters = {}
        for ak in tool_rag_agents:
            ad = get_adapter(ak, REPO_DIR)
            if not ad.setup():
                raise RuntimeError(f"FATAL: Failed to setup adapter {ak}")
            adapters[ak] = ad

        measured_tool_rag_records = []
        rng = random.Random(2026)

        try:
            for regime_key, cache_flag in [("regime_b_warm", "true"), ("regime_a_cold", "false")]:
                os.environ["BENCH_CACHE_PROMPT"] = cache_flag
                blocks = [(ak, w) for ak in tool_rag_agents for w in workloads]
                rng.shuffle(blocks)
                print(f"\n🚀 Executing Tool-RAG physical campaign for {regime_key} (BENCH_CACHE_PROMPT={cache_flag}, {len(blocks)} blocks)...")

                for b_idx, (ak, w) in enumerate(blocks, start=1):
                    ad = adapters[ak]
                    print(f"\n📦 [{regime_key} Block {b_idx:02d}/{len(blocks):02d}] {ak} on {w} ({n_warmups} warmups + {n_evals} evals)", flush=True)

                    for w_rep in range(1, n_warmups + 1):
                        res = ad.run_workload(w, iterations=1)
                        pt = int(res.get("prompt_tokens", 0))
                        ct = int(res.get("completion_tokens", res.get("output_tokens", 0)))
                        ttft_ms = float(res.get("ttft_ms", 0.0))
                        itl_ms = float(res.get("itl_ms", 0.0))
                        t_prune_ms = float(res.get("t_prune_ms", 0.0))
                        if pt <= 0 or ct <= 0 or ttft_ms <= 0 or itl_ms <= 0 or t_prune_ms <= 0:
                            raise RuntimeError(f"FATAL: Invalid Tool-RAG warmup telemetry: {res}")
                        t_task_ms = float(res.get("t_task_ms", 0.0))
                        t_neural_ms = float(res.get("t_neural_ms", 0.0))
                        t_orch_ms = float(res.get("t_orch_ms", max(0.0, t_task_ms - t_neural_ms)))
                        phi_orch = (t_orch_ms / t_task_ms * 100.0) if t_task_ms > 0 else 0.0
                        cached_t = int(res.get("cached_tokens", 0))
                        cache_hit = (cached_t / pt * 100.0) if pt > 0 else 0.0

                        measured_tool_rag_records.append({
                            "timestamp_iso": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
                            "regime": regime_key,
                            "agent_key": ak,
                            "architecture": ad.name,
                            "runtime": ad.runtime,
                            "mode": "tool_rag_top5_of_54",
                            "workload": w,
                            "rep": w_rep,
                            "is_warmup": True,
                            "is_simulated": False,
                            "data_source": f"measured_hardware_tool_rag_{regime_key}_warmup",
                            "telemetry_backend": smc_monitor.telemetry_backend,
                            "t_task_ms": round(t_task_ms, 2),
                            "t_neural_ms": round(t_neural_ms, 2),
                            "t_orch_ms": round(t_orch_ms, 3),
                            "phi_orch_pct": round(phi_orch, 3),
                            "ttft_ms": round(ttft_ms, 2),
                            "itl_ms": round(itl_ms, 2),
                            "t_dispatch_ms": round(float(res.get("t_dispatch_ms", 0.0)), 3),
                            "t_prune_ms": round(t_prune_ms, 4),
                            "t_http_ms": round(float(res.get("t_http_ms", 0.0)), 3),
                            "rss_peak_mb": round(float(res.get("rss_peak_mb", 0.0)), 2),
                            "proc_peak_phys_mb": round(float(res.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", 0.0)), 2),
                            "proc_instructions": int(res.get("proc_instructions", 0)),
                            "proc_cycles": int(res.get("proc_cycles", 0)),
                            "proc_billed_energy": int(res.get("proc_billed_energy", 0)),
                            "prompt_tokens": pt,
                            "completion_tokens": ct,
                            "cached_tokens": cached_t,
                            "uncached_prompt_tokens": int(res.get("uncached_prompt_tokens", 0)),
                            "cache_hit_pct": round(cache_hit, 2),
                            "energy_total_j": None,
                            "energy_net_above_idle_j": None,
                            "edp_js": None,
                        })

                    for eval_rep in range(1, n_evals + 1):
                        t0_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
                        res = ad.run_workload(w, iterations=1)
                        t1_ns = time.monotonic_ns()

                        energy_dict = smc_monitor.slice_energy(t0_ns, t1_ns)
                        if energy_dict is None:
                            time.sleep(0.06)
                            energy_dict = smc_monitor.slice_energy(t0_ns, t1_ns)
                        if energy_dict is None or energy_dict.get("energy_total_j") is None:
                            raise RuntimeError(f"FATAL: SMC slice_energy returned None on {ak} {w} rep {eval_rep}")

                        pt = int(res.get("prompt_tokens", 0))
                        ct = int(res.get("completion_tokens", res.get("output_tokens", 0)))
                        ttft_ms = float(res.get("ttft_ms", 0.0))
                        itl_ms = float(res.get("itl_ms", 0.0))
                        t_prune_ms = float(res.get("t_prune_ms", 0.0))
                        if pt <= 0 or ct <= 0 or ttft_ms <= 0 or itl_ms <= 0 or t_prune_ms <= 0:
                            raise RuntimeError(f"FATAL: Invalid Tool-RAG eval telemetry: {res}")

                        t_task_ms = float(res.get("t_task_ms", 0.0))
                        t_neural_ms = float(res.get("t_neural_ms", 0.0))
                        t_orch_ms = float(res.get("t_orch_ms", max(0.0, t_task_ms - t_neural_ms)))
                        phi_orch = (t_orch_ms / t_task_ms * 100.0) if t_task_ms > 0 else 0.0
                        cached_t = int(res.get("cached_tokens", 0))
                        cache_hit = (cached_t / pt * 100.0) if pt > 0 else 0.0

                        e_tot = float(energy_dict["energy_total_j"])
                        e_net = float(energy_dict["energy_net_above_idle_j"])
                        edp_js = round(e_net * (t_task_ms / 1000.0), 4)

                        rec = {
                            "timestamp_iso": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
                            "regime": regime_key,
                            "agent_key": ak,
                            "architecture": ad.name,
                            "runtime": ad.runtime,
                            "mode": "tool_rag_top5_of_54",
                            "workload": w,
                            "rep": eval_rep,
                            "is_warmup": False,
                            "is_simulated": False,
                            "data_source": f"measured_hardware_tool_rag_{regime_key}",
                            "telemetry_backend": smc_monitor.telemetry_backend,
                            "t_task_ms": round(t_task_ms, 2),
                            "t_neural_ms": round(t_neural_ms, 2),
                            "t_orch_ms": round(t_orch_ms, 3),
                            "phi_orch_pct": round(phi_orch, 3),
                            "ttft_ms": round(ttft_ms, 2),
                            "itl_ms": round(itl_ms, 2),
                            "t_dispatch_ms": round(float(res.get("t_dispatch_ms", 0.0)), 3),
                            "t_prune_ms": round(t_prune_ms, 4),
                            "t_http_ms": round(float(res.get("t_http_ms", 0.0)), 3),
                            "rss_peak_mb": round(float(res.get("rss_peak_mb", 0.0)), 2),
                            "proc_peak_phys_mb": round(float(res.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", 0.0)), 2),
                            "proc_instructions": int(res.get("proc_instructions", 0)),
                            "proc_cycles": int(res.get("proc_cycles", 0)),
                            "proc_billed_energy": int(res.get("proc_billed_energy", 0)),
                            "prompt_tokens": pt,
                            "completion_tokens": ct,
                            "cached_tokens": cached_t,
                            "uncached_prompt_tokens": int(res.get("uncached_prompt_tokens", 0)),
                            "cache_hit_pct": round(cache_hit, 2),
                            "energy_total_j": e_tot,
                            "energy_net_above_idle_j": e_net,
                            "edp_js": edp_js,
                            "http_status": int(res.get("http_status", 200)),
                        }
                        measured_tool_rag_records.append(rec)

                        if eval_rep % 10 == 0 or eval_rep == n_evals:
                            print(
                                f"   [Rep {eval_rep:02d}/{n_evals:02d}] {ak:18s} {w}: Task={rec['t_task_ms']:6.1f}ms, "
                                f"Prune={rec['t_prune_ms']:5.3f}ms, Prompt={pt}, NetE={e_net:.2f}J",
                                flush=True,
                            )
        finally:
            smc_monitor.stop_campaign()

        ref_a = load_reference_baseline_trials(RAW_TRIALS_PATH, "regime_a_cold")
        ref_b = load_reference_baseline_trials(WARM_TRIALS_PATH, "regime_b_warm")
        all_records = ref_a + ref_b + measured_tool_rag_records

        with open(OUT_TRIALS_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for r in all_records:
                f.write(json.dumps(r) + "\n")
        print(f"\n💾 Wrote {len(all_records)} total records to {OUT_TRIALS_PATH}")

    # Summarize across regimes, workloads, and arms
    summary: dict[str, Any] = {
        "description": "Zero-Copy Dynamic Tool-RAG Schema Pruner (54 -> Top-5 Tools) Empirical Evaluation on Apple Silicon M4",
        "regime_a_cold": {},
        "regime_b_warm": {},
    }

    for reg_key in ("regime_a_cold", "regime_b_warm"):
        summary[reg_key] = {}
        for w in workloads:
            summary[reg_key][w] = {}
            for ak in all_agents:
                sub = [
                    r for r in all_records
                    if r.get("regime") == reg_key and r.get("workload") == w and r.get("agent_key") == ak and not r.get("is_warmup", False)
                ]
                if not sub:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"FATAL: Missing evaluated records for {reg_key} {w} {ak}")
                tasks = [float(r["t_task_ms"]) for r in sub]
                orchs = [float(r["t_orch_ms"]) for r in sub]
                prunes = [float(r.get("t_prune_ms", 0.0)) for r in sub]
                pts = [float(r["prompt_tokens"]) for r in sub]
                enets = [float(r["energy_net_above_idle_j"]) for r in sub if r.get("energy_net_above_idle_j") is not None]
                edps = [float(r.get("edp_js") or r.get("edp_net_js") or (float(r["energy_net_above_idle_j"]) * float(r["t_task_ms"]) / 1000.0)) for r in sub if r.get("energy_net_above_idle_j") is not None]
                phys = [float(r.get("proc_peak_phys_mb", 0.0)) for r in sub]

                summary[reg_key][w][ak] = {
                    "n_trials": len(sub),
                    "prompt_tokens_mean": round(_mean(pts), 1),
                    "t_prune_mean_ms": round(_mean(prunes), 4),
                    "t_task_mean_ms": round(_mean(tasks), 2),
                    "t_task_median_ms": round(_median(tasks), 2),
                    "t_task_std_ms": round(_std(tasks), 2),
                    "t_orch_mean_ms": round(_mean(orchs), 3),
                    "energy_net_mean_j": round(_mean(enets), 2),
                    "edp_mean_js": round(_mean(edps), 2),
                    "proc_peak_phys_mb_mean": round(_mean(phys), 2),
                }

            # Compute relative reductions vs openclaw_full
            full_s = summary[reg_key][w]["openclaw_full"]
            min_s = summary[reg_key][w]["openclaw_minimal"]
            for rag_ak in ("openclaw_tool_rag", "picoclaw_tool_rag"):
                rag_s = summary[reg_key][w][rag_ak]
                rag_s["latency_reduction_vs_full_54_pct"] = round(
                    (1.0 - rag_s["t_task_median_ms"] / full_s["t_task_median_ms"]) * 100.0, 2
                )
                rag_s["energy_reduction_vs_full_54_pct"] = round(
                    (1.0 - rag_s["energy_net_mean_j"] / full_s["energy_net_mean_j"]) * 100.0, 2
                )
                rag_s["edp_reduction_vs_full_54_pct"] = round(
                    (1.0 - rag_s["edp_mean_js"] / full_s["edp_mean_js"]) * 100.0, 2
                )
                rag_s["latency_reduction_vs_minimal_15_pct"] = round(
                    (1.0 - rag_s["t_task_median_ms"] / min_s["t_task_median_ms"]) * 100.0, 2
                )

    with open(OUT_SUMMARY_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)
    print(f"✅ Saved Tool-RAG summary JSON: {OUT_SUMMARY_PATH}")

    generate_latex_table(summary)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

================================================================================
END FILE: scripts/run_tool_rag_mitigation_experiment.py
================================================================================

FILE: scripts/verify_manifest.py
SHA-256: 776c66609e5f5c05abe685923b0a22b249dc20cfc7e29e261405b1807ac363d2

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
scripts/verify_manifest.py - Cryptographic Artifact Integrity Verifier
Verificador Criptográfico de Integridade de Artefatos

Validates registered SHA-256 hashes in data/manifest_sha256.json against files on disk.
Valida os hashes SHA-256 registrados em data/manifest_sha256.json contra os arquivos em disco.

Author / Autor: Rafael Paniccia Cassetta (UFABC)
Language / Idioma: Bilingual (PT-BR & EN-US)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import sys

REPO_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
MANIFEST_PATH = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "data", "manifest_sha256.json")


def compute_sha256(filepath: str) -> str:
    """Compute SHA-256 hex digest of a file in 64 KB streaming chunks."""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(filepath, "rb") as f:
        while chunk := f.read(65536):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def update_manifest() -> int:
    """Recomputes SHA-256 for all tracked files and updates data/manifest_sha256.json."""
    if not os.path.exists(MANIFEST_PATH):
        print(f"❌ [ERROR] Manifest file not found: {MANIFEST_PATH}")
        return 1

    with open(MANIFEST_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        manifest = json.load(f)

    # Also track additional core files if present
    additional = [
        "paper/RESPONSE_TO_REVIEWERS.md",
        "benchmark/protocol_invariants.py",
        "benchmark/bun_jsc_agent.mjs",
        "benchmark/adapters/bun_minimal_adapter.py",
        "scripts/run_prefill_ablation.py",
        "scripts/run_w2_resample.py",
        "scripts/run_warm_cache_campaign.py",
        "scripts/run_regime_a_equalized_update.py",
        "scripts/run_regime_b_w2_w4_unified_session.py",
        "scripts/run_inter_session_replication.py",
        "scripts/run_tool_rag_mitigation_experiment.py",
        "scripts/export_audit_bundle.py",
        "results/exploratory_campaigns/raw_trials_pre_w2_fix.jsonl",
        "results/exploratory_campaigns/warm_cache_trials_two_session_backup.jsonl",
        "results/publication_campaign/prefill_regression_fit.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/tables/table_prefill_ablation.tex",
        "results/publication_campaign/tables/table_warm_cache.tex",
        "results/publication_campaign/tables/table_tool_rag_mitigation.tex",
        "results/publication_campaign/warm_cache_trials.jsonl",
        "results/publication_campaign/warm_cache_summary.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/warm_cache_statistical_tests.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/campaign_metadata.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/spearman_correlation_matrix.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/tables/table_spearman_correlation.tex",
        "results/publication_campaign/pca_variance_decomposition.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/tables/table_pca_loadings.tex",
        "results/publication_campaign/prefill_ablation.jsonl",
        "results/publication_campaign/inter_session_trials.jsonl",
        "results/publication_campaign/inter_session_replication.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/tool_rag_mitigation_trials.jsonl",
        "results/publication_campaign/tool_rag_mitigation_summary.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/hyperfine_cold_start.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/hyperfine_agent_init.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/hyperfine_worker_version.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/llama_server_publication.log",
        "results/publication_campaign/powermetrics_publication_sample.txt",
        "scripts/run_uma_energy_and_bandwidth_attribution.py",
        "scripts/run_kv_cache_thrashing_ablation.py",
        "scripts/run_independent_validation_suite.py",
        "results/publication_campaign/uma_energy_bandwidth_trials.jsonl",
        "results/publication_campaign/uma_energy_bandwidth_attribution.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/kv_cache_thrashing_trials.jsonl",
        "results/publication_campaign/kv_cache_thrashing_summary.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/sensitivity_and_outlier_analysis.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/independent_validation_report.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/cross_hardware_and_independent_validation.json",
        "results/publication_campaign/tables/table_energy_bandwidth_deconvolution.tex",
        "data/roofline_kv_affine_model.json",
        "data/harness_self_overhead.json",
        "data/external_framework_langchain_summary.json",
        "extensions/langchain_adapter/langchain_orch.py",
        "src/telemetry_backend.py",
        "scripts/analyze_roofline_and_kv_scaling.py",
        "scripts/measure_harness_self_overhead.py",
        "scripts/run_cross_hardware_replica.py",
        "scripts/run_cross_hardware_replica.sh",
        "scripts/run_external_framework_langchain_benchmark.py",
    ]
    for rel_path in additional:
        abs_path = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, rel_path)
        if os.path.exists(abs_path) and rel_path not in manifest:
            manifest[rel_path] = ""

    updated = {}
    for rel_path in sorted(manifest.keys()):
        if (
            rel_path.startswith("results/simulated_check/")
            or rel_path == "data/results_all.json"
            or rel_path.startswith("results/exploratory_campaigns/campaign_comparison/")
            or "poisson" in rel_path.lower()
            or "fig7" in rel_path
            or "fig8" in rel_path
        ):
            continue
        abs_path = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, rel_path)
        if os.path.exists(abs_path):
            updated[rel_path] = compute_sha256(abs_path)
        else:
            print(f"⚠️  [SKIP] File missing on disk, omitting from updated manifest: {rel_path}")

    with open(MANIFEST_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(updated, f, indent=2)
        f.write("\n")

    print(f"✅ [SUCCESS] Updated {len(updated)} tracked file signatures in {MANIFEST_PATH}")
    return 0


def verify_manifest() -> int:
    if "--update" in sys.argv:
        return update_manifest()

    if not os.path.exists(MANIFEST_PATH):
        print(f"❌ [ERROR] Manifest file not found: {MANIFEST_PATH}")
        return 1

    with open(MANIFEST_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        manifest = json.load(f)

    total_files = len(manifest)
    print("========================================================================")
    print(" 🔐 [PT-BR] Verificação de Integridade Criptográfica (SHA-256)")
    print(f"    [EN-US] Cryptographic Artifact Integrity Verification ({total_files} files)")
    print("========================================================================")

    mismatches = []
    missing = []
    verified_count = 0

    for rel_path, exp_val in manifest.items():
        abs_path = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, rel_path)
        if not os.path.exists(abs_path):
            missing.append(rel_path)
            continue

        if isinstance(exp_val, dict):
            expected_hash = exp_val.get("sha256", "")
        else:
            expected_hash = str(exp_val)

        actual_hash = compute_sha256(abs_path)
        if actual_hash.lower() != expected_hash.lower():
            mismatches.append((rel_path, expected_hash, actual_hash))
        else:
            verified_count += 1

    if missing:
        print(f"\n❌ [ERROR] Missing files ({len(missing)}):")
        for m in missing:
            print(f"   - {m}")

    if mismatches:
        print(f"\n❌ [ERROR] Hash mismatches ({len(mismatches)}):")
        for p, exp, act in mismatches:
            print(f"   - {p}")
            print(f"     Expected: {exp}")
            print(f"     Actual:   {act}")

    if not missing and not mismatches:
        print(f"✅ [SUCCESS] All {verified_count}/{total_files} tracked files match their cryptographic SHA-256 signature!")
        print("   [PT-BR] Todos os arquivos rastreados conferem com suas assinaturas SHA-256 registradas.")
        print("========================================================================\n")
        return 0
    else:
        print(f"\n⚠️  [FAILURE] Verification failed: {verified_count}/{total_files} valid, {len(mismatches)} altered, {len(missing)} missing.")
        print("========================================================================\n")
        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(verify_manifest())

================================================================================
END FILE: scripts/verify_manifest.py
================================================================================

# Guia Didático e Tutorial Visual Passo a Passo / Beginner's Walkthrough & Visual Proofs

[![Platform](https://img.shields.io/badge/Platform-Apple%20Silicon%20M4-purple.svg)](https://www.apple.com/)
[![Bilingual](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-PT--BR%20%7C%20EN--US-blue.svg)](#)
[![License](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-MIT-black.svg)](../LICENSE)

---

## 🇧🇷 Português (PT-BR): Como Entender e Reproduzir em 3 Minutos

Este guia didático foi elaborado para estudantes, pesquisadores e engenheiros que desejam compreender a essência do benchmark sem atrito técnico imediato, acompanhando cada etapa com exemplos ilustrativos da estrutura de saída do terminal no Apple Silicon M4.

---

### 0. Como Reproduzir em Qualquer Máquina (Linux, Windows WSL, macOS)

Você **NÃO precisa de um Mac com Apple Silicon nem de GPU dedicada** para reproduzir e auditar este trabalho científico! Qualquer estudante ou revisor em Linux (Ubuntu, Debian, Fedora), Windows (via WSL2) ou macOS Intel pode executar o pipeline completo:

```bash
# 1. Clonar e entrar na pasta do projeto
git clone https://github.com/drsigma/picoclaw-vs-openclaw-benchmark.git
cd picoclaw-vs-openclaw-benchmark

# 2. Configurar o ambiente isolado com 1 comando
make setup

# 3. Executar a Verificação Rápida Portátil (< 5 segundos, sem GPU, sem Sudo)
make dry-run

# 4. Executar os 32 testes unitários automatizados (pytest e linter ruff)
make test

# 5. Regenerar todas as 6 figuras científicas em 300 DPI
make reproduce-figures
```

*Caso você possua um Mac com Apple Silicon (M1/M2/M3/M4):*
* Execute `make benchmark` para rodar a campanha física real em espaço de usuário comum;
* Execute `make benchmark-full-smc` caso queira amostragem física de miliwatts com privilégios de superusuário via Apple SMC.

---

### 1. A Analogia Central: O Carro e os Dois Motoristas

Para compreender este experimento em 30 segundos, imagine um veículo de corrida:
* **O Motor do Carro:** É o modelo de Inteligência Artificial da Meta (**Llama-3.2 de 3 bilhões de parâmetros**). Ele processa a linguagem e gera as palavras.
* **O Suporte do Motor:** É o **`llama.cpp`**, que conecta esse motor diretamente à placa de vídeo Metal de 10 núcleos do Mac mini M4.
* **Os Dois Motoristas (Orquestradores de Agentes):**
  1. **OpenClaw (Node.js / TypeScript):** Carrega uma mochila pesada contendo 54 esquemas de ferramentas corporativas e necessita de um tradutor simultâneo (a máquina virtual Google V8) para se comunicar com o processador. É flexível e modular, mas consome muita memória RAM e energia térmica.
  2. **PicoClaw (Go):** Um atleta minimalista compilado estaticamente. Fala a linguagem nativa ARM64 do Mac mini, não necessita de interpretador e consome memória mínima.

**A Pergunta de Pesquisa:** Sob o mesmo motor neural local idêntico, qual motorista completa a tarefa com menor latência, menor consumo de memória RAM e maior eficiência energética?

---

### 2. O Papel dos Datasets em `data/workload_data/`

Para que as medições não se limitassem a diálogos puramente sintéticos, os agentes realizam operações reais de computação sobre arquivos em disco:
* **`mock_files/` (50 arquivos):** Consumido nos testes **W2** e **W4**. O agente executa a ferramenta `list_dir` para inspecionar, contar e ler metadados de arquivos.
* **`system_logs.jsonl` (750 registros JSON):** Consumido nos testes **W2** e **W4**. O agente usa a ferramenta `grep_logs` para buscar erros graves (`CRITICAL`/`ERROR`) e depois a ferramenta `calc_stats` para calcular médias e desvios de latência do barramento UMA.
* **`rag_chunks_10k.json` e `embedding_head_100k.npz`:** Consumidos no teste **W3 (Vector RAG)**. O agente carrega esses documentos em memória e calcula similaridade de cosseno ponderada de 384 dimensões para ranquear os textos semanticamente mais próximos.

---

### 3. Passo a Passo e Exemplos Ilustrativos da Estrutura de Saída (Illustrative Runner Output Format)

#### Passo 1: Inicialização do Servidor Neural Metal GPU (`llama-server`)
O servidor neural aloca os tensores do Llama-3.2-3B na memória unificada (UMA) e carrega os shaders na GPU Metal do Mac mini:
```bash
./tools/llama_cpp/llama-b11887/llama-server \
  -m models/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf \
  -c 32768 -np 1 -ngl 99 --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8080 -t 4
```
```text
0.04.154.161 I srv  llama_server: initializing ...
0.04.154.169 I cmn  common_param: common_params_print_info: verbosity = 3 (adjust with the `-lv N` CLI arg)
0.04.156.027 I srv    load_model: loading model './models/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf'
0.05.841.379 I cmn          init: llama threadpool init, n_threads = 4
0.06.149.561 I srv    load_model: initializing, n_slots = 1, n_ctx_slot = 32768, kv_unified = 'false'
0.06.152.620 I srv  server_run_i: model loaded
0.06.152.626 I srv  server_on_li: listening on http://127.0.0.1:8080
```
*Fonte do Log Real / Real Log Source:* `results/publication_campaign/llama_server_publication.log` (Linhas 1–8).  
*Legenda Técnica:* Inicialização do `llama-server` (b11887) no Apple M4 (`Mac16,10`). Observa-se a alocação de 3.21 bilhões de parâmetros (2,019 GB em Q4_K_M) diretamente na DRAM compartilhada (`n_slots = 1, n_ctx_slot = 32768`) e prontidão do endpoint HTTP loopback.

---

#### Passo 2: Verificação e Validação dos Datasets dos Workloads
Antes de iniciar os testes, inspeciona-se a integridade física dos datasets determinísticos:
```bash
ls -lh data/workload_data/
head -n 5 data/workload_data/system_logs.jsonl
```
```text
data/workload_data/
├── mock_files/ (50 arquivos sintéticos determinísticos)
├── system_logs.jsonl (750 linhas estruturadas de log APFS)
├── rag_chunks_10k.json (10.000 fragmentos textuais para busca vetorial)
├── doc_embeddings.bin (matriz binária float32 de embeddings 10.000 x 384)
├── w_proj.bin (matriz de projeção float32 256 x 384)
└── b_proj.bin (vetor de bias float32 de 384 dimensões)
```
*Legenda Técnica:* Inspeção dos 50 arquivos sintéticos, do log estruturado de 750 linhas APFS e dos pesos de projeção vetorial em float32 (`doc_embeddings.bin`, `w_proj.bin`).

---

#### Passo 3: Execução Física dos Agentes em Tempo Real
Os orquestradores executam os testes fatoriais W1 a W4 contra o servidor neural:
```bash
./tools/.venv/bin/python benchmark/run_unified_benchmark.py
```
*(Exemplo Ilustrativo da Estrutura de Saída do Runner / Illustrative Runner Output Format)*
```text
[001/600] picoclaw         W1 Rep 1: Task=5497.0ms, Orch=29.73ms, RAM=11.2MB, EDP=491.2J·s
[002/600] openclaw_minimal W1 Rep 1: Task=5847.5ms, Orch=51.28ms, RAM=85.6MB, EDP=550.5J·s
[003/600] openclaw_full    W1 Rep 1: Task=20674.1ms, Orch=66.44ms, RAM=85.6MB, EDP=6815.0J·s
[004/600] python_native    W1 Rep 1: Task=5485.6ms, Orch=10.41ms, RAM=38.2MB, EDP=492.2J·s
[005/600] neural_baseline  W1 Rep 1: Task=5489.6ms, Orch=0.00ms, RAM=0.0MB, EDP=483.6J·s
```
*Legenda Técnica:* Traces em tempo real ilustrando a estrutura de saída do runner com a latência de tarefa ($T_{\text{task}}$), tempo de inferência neural ($T_{\text{neural}}$), overhead do runtime ($T_{\text{orch}}$) e consumo de memória RAM residente (RSS).

---

#### Passo 4: Medição Estatística de Cold-Start com Hyperfine
Cronometragem com precisão nanométrica do tempo de disparo a frio (30 repetições, `hyperfine_cold_start.json` e `hyperfine_worker_version.json`):
```bash
./tools/hyperfine/hyperfine --warmup 5 --runs 30 \
  "./tools/picoclaw/picoclaw version" \
  "./tools/node/bin/node ./benchmark/openclaw_orch.mjs --version"
```
```text
Benchmark 1: ./tools/picoclaw/picoclaw version
  Time (mean ± σ):       9.74 ms ±   0.38 ms    [Median: 9.67 ms]
  Range (min … max):     9.12 ms …  10.84 ms    30 runs
Benchmark 2: ./tools/node/bin/node ./benchmark/openclaw_orch.mjs --version
  Time (mean ± σ):      37.15 ms ±   4.12 ms    [Median: 33.48 ms]
  Range (min … max):    32.80 ms …  48.90 ms    30 runs
Summary:
  PicoClaw ran 3.81 times faster (mean) and 3.46 times faster (median) than OpenClaw
```
*Fonte do Log Real / Real Log Source:* `results/publication_campaign/hyperfine_cold_start.json` (e `results/publication_campaign/hyperfine_worker_version.json`).  
*Legenda Técnica:* No par equivalente de checagem de versão (`hyperfine_cold_start.json`), o PicoClaw (Go nativo) inicializa em **$9{,}74\text{ ms}$** de média (**$9{,}67\text{ ms}$** de mediana) vs. **$37{,}15\text{ ms}$** de média (**$33{,}48\text{ ms}$** de mediana) no OpenClaw (Node.js/V8), comprovando vantagem de inicialização de **$3{,}81\times$ na média ($3{,}46\times$ na mediana)** a favor do Go (e **$3{,}04\text{ ms}$ vs. $22{,}15\text{ ms}$ [$7{,}29\times$]** nos workers isomórficos em `hyperfine_worker_version.json`).

## 🇺🇸 English (EN-US): How to Understand and Reproduce in 3 Minutes

This walkthrough is tailored for students, researchers, and engineers seeking an intuitive yet technically precise understanding of the benchmark, supported by illustrative terminal output examples recorded on Apple Silicon M4.

---

### 0. How to Reproduce on Any Machine (Linux, Windows WSL, macOS)

You **DO NOT need an Apple Silicon Mac or a dedicated GPU** to reproduce and audit this research! Any reviewer, student, or researcher on Linux (Ubuntu, Debian, Fedora), Windows (via WSL2), or Intel/Apple Silicon macOS can run the entire pipeline:

```bash
# 1. Clone repository and enter project directory
git clone https://github.com/drsigma/picoclaw-vs-openclaw-benchmark.git
cd picoclaw-vs-openclaw-benchmark

# 2. Setup isolated environment with a single command
make setup

# 3. Run Instant Portable Dry-Run Verification (< 5 seconds, No GPU, No Sudo)
make dry-run

# 4. Run the 32 automated unit tests (pytest and ruff linter)
make test

# 5. Re-render all 6 publication figures in 300 DPI
make reproduce-figures
```

*If you are running on an Apple Silicon Mac (M1/M2/M3/M4):*
* Run `make benchmark` for physical execution in standard user space (no sudo required);
* Run `make benchmark-full-smc` for complete physical hardware electrical telemetry via Apple SMC shunts.

---

### 1. The Core Metaphor: The Car and the Two Drivers

To grasp this research benchmark in 30 seconds, imagine a racecar:
* **The Engine:** Meta's local **Llama-3.2 (3-billion parameter)** model generating tokens and reasoning.
* **The Engine Mount:** **`llama.cpp`**, connecting the neural weights directly to Apple Silicon M4's 10-core Metal GPU.
* **The Two Drivers (Agent Orchestrators):**
  1. **OpenClaw (Node.js / TypeScript):** A driver carrying an enterprise backpack filled with 54 tool schemas, requiring Google V8's live JIT interpreter to talk to the hardware. Highly flexible, but incurs elevated RAM and thermal dissipation.
  2. **PicoClaw (Go):** A compiled minimalist athlete speaking native Mach-O ARM64 machine code with zero interpreter overhead.

**The Research Question:** Under an identical local neural serving baseline, which runtime architecture achieves lower latency, reduced RAM footprint, and superior energy efficiency?

---

### 2. Workload Datasets in `data/workload_data/`

To ground evaluation in realistic operating system activity beyond conversational queries, agents process deterministic datasets:
* **`mock_files/` (50 files):** Evaluated in **W2** and **W4**. The agent calls `list_dir` to inspect disk files, parse attributes, and verify permissions.
* **`system_logs.jsonl` (750 JSON records):** Evaluated in **W2** and **W4**. The agent calls `grep_logs` to filter severe error entries and invokes `calc_stats` to compute rolling latency metrics.
* **`rag_chunks_10k.json` & `embedding_head_100k.npz`:** Evaluated in **W3 (Vector RAG)**. The agent performs in-memory 384-dimensional cosine similarity ranking across 10,000 document text chunks.

---

### 3. Step-by-Step Walkthrough with Illustrative Runner Output Format

#### Step 1: Launch Local Metal GPU Neural Server (`llama-server`)
```bash
./tools/llama_cpp/llama-b11887/llama-server \
  -m models/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf \
  -c 32768 -np 1 -ngl 99 --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8080 -t 4
```
```text
0.04.154.161 I srv  llama_server: initializing ...
0.04.154.169 I cmn  common_param: common_params_print_info: verbosity = 3 (adjust with the `-lv N` CLI arg)
0.04.156.027 I srv    load_model: loading model './models/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf'
0.05.841.379 I cmn          init: llama threadpool init, n_threads = 4
0.06.149.561 I srv    load_model: initializing, n_slots = 1, n_ctx_slot = 32768, kv_unified = 'false'
0.06.152.620 I srv  server_run_i: model loaded
0.06.152.626 I srv  server_on_li: listening on http://127.0.0.1:8080
```
*Real Log Source:* `results/publication_campaign/llama_server_publication.log` (Lines 1–8).  
*Technical Caption:* Initialization of `llama-server` (build b11887) on Apple M4 (`Mac16,10`). Notice the allocation of 3.21B parameters (2.019 GB in Q4_K_M) directly in shared DRAM (`n_slots = 1, n_ctx_slot = 32768`) and endpoint readiness on HTTP loopback.

---

#### Step 2: Inspect Workload Datasets
```bash
ls -lh data/workload_data/
head -n 5 data/workload_data/system_logs.jsonl
```
```text
data/workload_data/
├── mock_files/ (50 synthetic deterministic text files)
├── system_logs.jsonl (750 APFS structured JSON log lines)
├── rag_chunks_10k.json (10,000 vector chunks)
├── doc_embeddings.bin (10,000 x 384 float32 embeddings)
├── w_proj.bin (256 x 384 float32 projection head)
└── b_proj.bin (384 float32 bias vector)
```
*Technical Caption:* Inspection of 50 mock files, 750 APFS structured JSON log lines, and dense float32 projection matrix (`doc_embeddings.bin`, `w_proj.bin`).

---

#### Step 3: Run Master Live Benchmark Campaign
```bash
./tools/.venv/bin/python benchmark/run_unified_benchmark.py
```
*(Illustrative Runner Output Format / Exemplo Ilustrativo da Estrutura de Saída do Runner)*
```text
[001/600] picoclaw         W1 Rep 1: Task=5497.0ms, Orch=29.73ms, RAM=11.2MB, EDP=491.2J·s
[002/600] openclaw_minimal W1 Rep 1: Task=5847.5ms, Orch=51.28ms, RAM=85.6MB, EDP=550.5J·s
[003/600] openclaw_full    W1 Rep 1: Task=20674.1ms, Orch=66.44ms, RAM=85.6MB, EDP=6815.0J·s
[004/600] python_native    W1 Rep 1: Task=5485.6ms, Orch=10.41ms, RAM=38.2MB, EDP=492.2J·s
[005/600] neural_baseline  W1 Rep 1: Task=5489.6ms, Orch=0.00ms, RAM=0.0MB, EDP=483.6J·s
```
*Technical Caption:* Real-time execution traces demonstrating the illustrative runner output format capturing task latency ($T_{\text{task}}$), neural serving time ($T_{\text{neural}}$), runtime overhead ($T_{\text{orch}}$), and Resident Set Size (RSS).

#### Step 4: Measure Cold-Start Launch Latency with Hyperfine
```bash
./tools/hyperfine/hyperfine --warmup 5 --runs 30 \
  "./tools/picoclaw/picoclaw version" \
  "./tools/node/bin/node ./benchmark/openclaw_orch.mjs --version"
```
```text
Benchmark 1: ./tools/picoclaw/picoclaw version
  Time (mean ± σ):       9.74 ms ±   0.38 ms    [Median: 9.67 ms]
  Range (min … max):     9.12 ms …  10.84 ms    30 runs

  Time (mean ± σ):      37.15 ms ±   4.12 ms    [Median: 33.48 ms]
  Range (min … max):    32.80 ms …  48.90 ms    30 runs
Summary:
  PicoClaw ran 3.81 times faster (mean) and 3.46 times faster (median) than OpenClaw
```
*Real Log Source:* `results/publication_campaign/hyperfine_cold_start.json` (and `results/publication_campaign/hyperfine_worker_version.json`).  
*Technical Caption:* Hyperfine statistical benchmarking (`hyperfine_cold_start.json`) proving PicoClaw launches in **$9.74\text{ ms}$** mean (**$9.67\text{ ms}$** median) vs. OpenClaw's **$37.15\text{ ms}$** mean (**$33.48\text{ ms}$** median), a **$3.81\times$ mean ($3.46\times$ median)** speedup (and **$3.04\text{ ms}$ vs. $22.15\text{ ms}$ [$7.29\times$]** across the isomorphic workers in `hyperfine_worker_version.json`).
